# Agents · R · part 29

1,117 entries, reviewer to reviewer-agent. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **reviewer**: Final gate before the human. Reviews the feature branch against the spec and tests for correctness, scope, safety, and quality, verifies the suite is green, and produces an APPROVE / REQUEST-CHANGES verdict plus a PR-ready summary. Use after implementer. Read-only on code; never merges or pushes. ([Muhanad-husn/AEO](https://github.com/Muhanad-husn/AEO/blob/HEAD/source/global-workspace/iteration-2/eval-0/without_skill/run-0/outputs/it2-e0-base/ai-enterprise-template/.claude/agents/reviewer.md))
- **reviewer**: Accepts: spec path + PR number from orchestrator. Rejects immediately (return blocked): freeform review requests, note content, world-building tasks. ([mumoryan/loci](https://github.com/mumoryan/loci/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/reviewer.md))
- **reviewer**: Senior code reviewer for quality assurance and verification. Reviews code against specs, checks for anti-patterns, validates security, and produces structured verification reports with severity-ranked findings. ([murillodutt/cellm](https://github.com/murillodutt/cellm/blob/HEAD/cellm/agents/reviewer.md))
- **reviewer**: Salt-okunur kod incelemesi — diff'i proje tuzaklarına karşı doğrular, bulgularını lead'e raporlar. ([musaay/idealode](https://github.com/musaay/idealode/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/reviewer.md))
- **reviewer**: Reviews a diff for correctness. Use after a change is made. - **reviewer**: Reviews a diff for correctness. Triggered by: review, audit. ([musher-dev/engineering-conventions](https://github.com/musher-dev/engineering-conventions/blob/HEAD/engineering-conventions/tests/fixtures/repos/agent-09-passes-quoted-description/.claude/agents/reviewer.md))

- **reviewer**: Blind code reviewer for the senior-dev workflow. Use only when the senior-dev skill dispatches it with a filled reviewer brief, not for general code review. ([Mustafa212/senior-dev](https://github.com/Mustafa212/senior-dev/blob/HEAD/agents/reviewer.md))

- **reviewer**: Architectural code reviewer for gitban sprint cards. Evaluates for tech debt, coupling, ADR violations, and TDD/IaC/DaC compliance. ([muunkky/hottopoteto](https://github.com/muunkky/hottopoteto/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/reviewer.md))
- **reviewer**: Used only by the beads-workflow run-bead and run-epic skills; not for general code review. ([muzomer/plugins](https://github.com/muzomer/plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/beads-workflow/agents/reviewer.md))
- **reviewer**: Use after @builder finishes work, before any merge. Checks for security issues, scope violations, test coverage gaps, and performance concerns. Does not modify code — only produces structured review output with CRITICAL / WARNING / SUGGESTION severities. ([mwathiben/PropManager](https://github.com/mwathiben/PropManager/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/reviewer.md))
- **reviewer**: Review step of a AI flow run. A fresh reviewer that never wrote the code reads the whole change against plan.md and the project's written rules, runs the tests itself and returns PASS or FAIL. Every blocking finding names a failing input or quotes a rule. Never edits source. ([MykhailoMishchenko/ai-flow](https://github.com/MykhailoMishchenko/ai-flow/blob/HEAD/plugins/ai-flow/agents/reviewer.md))
